5. Data Quality Audit

Audit TypeCountDescription
Bandwidth Normalization16270Raw bandwidth values, normalized MHz values, and validation issues
Coordinate Anomalies2Missing, out-of-range, or zero-sentinel coordinates
Identity Duplicates756Exact duplicate records and conflicting duplicate keys
Identity Anomalies8298Performance records not matched to engineering cells
Performance Anomalies0Missing, invalid-date, or non-numeric PRB/load records

6. Metric Definitions

1. Only strong same-coverage and strong opposite-coverage balance candidates are shown, with same-coverage first.

2. Hard expansion is sorted by descending source maximum load; reduction is sorted by ascending weighted merged load, then descending source maximum load.

3. For reduction, the source cell retains capacity and the candidate cell is evaluated for reduction.

4. High-/low-load valid days are total valid PRB-record days meeting the threshold, not the longest consecutive duration.

5. distance_m=0 for SN matching means logical co-location, not measured distance.

6. FREQ is a pass-through frequency, center-frequency, or custom identifier; no MHz unit is added.
